- **pause**: `crabbox pause` pauses a single lease, freeing the remote compute while preserving the sandbox's state, so it can be brought back later with [`resume`](resume.md). - **pause**: **Project-first approach**: - **If active project exists**: `projects/active/[project-name]/paused-contexts/YYYY-MM-DD-HH-MM-[description].md` - **Otherwise**: `.claude/paused-contexts/YYYY-MM-DD-HH-MM-[description].md` ([dylpickledev/claude-analytics-framework](https://github.com/dylpickledev/claude-analytics-framework/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pause.md) · ★4)
- **pause**: SOFT-PARK the current chat — a temporary pause with the intent to CONTINUE IN THIS SAME CHAT. Bring the task in flight to a logical stopping point, verify the tree is green, park neatly WITHOUT the heavy wrap-up (no push, no STATUS/README ceremony) and post a precise parking note in the chat. Use when the human says "pause", "park it", "hold on, back soon", "пауза", "припаркуйся", "прервёмся ненадолго". For the FULL session closure (STATUS, commits, pushes, handoff to other chats) use /end-chat-soft (unhurried) or /end-chat-force (urgent) instead. Trigger aliases (ru): «пауза», «сделаем паузу… ([MikalaiKryvusha/KAGO](https://github.com/MikalaiKryvusha/KAGO/blob/HEAD/.roo/commands/pause.md) · ★4 · 6 copies)
- **pause**: МЯГКАЯ ПАРКОВКА текущего чата — временная пауза с намерением ПРОДОЛЖИТЬ В ЭТОМ ЖЕ ЧАТЕ. Довести задачу в работе до логической точки, убедиться что дерево зелёное, припарковаться БЕЗ тяжёлой церемонии (без пуша, без ритуала STATUS/README) и оставить в чате точную парковочную заметку. Используй когда Криник говорит "пауза", "сделаем паузу", "припаркуйся", "прервёмся ненадолго", "отойду ненадолго", "pause", "park it". Для ПОЛНОГО закрытия сессии (STATUS, коммиты, пуш, передача эстафеты другим чатам) — навык /end-chat-soft (неторопливо) или /end-chat-force (срочно). ([MikalaiKryvusha/KrinikCam](https://github.com/MikalaiKryvusha/KrinikCam/blob/HEAD/.roo/commands/pause.md) · ★4)

- **pause**: The founder can be tired at any point. An idle session costs nothing, but its prompt cache expires within minutes — typing into a long stale session re-reads the entire conversation uncached at full price, every message. The cheap path is always: checkpoint → close → resume FRESH. This command is the deterministic button for that ritual. (Tired phrasing — "sleepy", "stopping", "continue tomorrow" ([drshailesh88/build_playbook](https://github.com/drshailesh88/build_playbook/blob/HEAD/commands/pause.md))
- **pause**: Pause cleanly — shut down agents, decide on build/test verification, save state + memory, run drift-check, commit and (by default) push. "local" skips push; "land" runs CI checks, closes issues, and ships — ff-merging in solo mode, opening a PR in team mode. "after" waits for in-flight agents to finish first; "sleep" puts the Mac to sleep once the pause fully succeeds. ([flykit-cc/flow](https://github.com/flykit-cc/flow/blob/HEAD/commands/pause.md))

- **perf**: Latency is measured from the planned arrival instant under open-loop Poisson arrivals, so a stall shows up as latency. [Measured performance](running.md#measured-performance) walks a run phase by phase and explains the record. - **perma-emit**: Emit a Permanence event from THIS project to other open sessions — a semi-real-time note that something interesting happened here, delivered into other projects' context (never their files). - **perma-help**: What this Permanence can do — every installed /perma-* command, and which background machinery is actually switched on for this machine. - **perma-list**: List every registered Permanence stream — name, what it is, when it was last touched, and its real-world path if it has one. Read-only. ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-list.md) · ★1)

- **perma-register**: Register an existing project as a Permanence stream — read its README, create the stream (seeded from the README), and add it to the registry. ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-register.md) · ★1)

- **perma-shutdown**: End-of-day wind-down — capture where things stand into this project's Permanence stream (PROJECT + QUESTIONS) and hand back a Done / in-flight / first-move-tomorrow summary, so the open loops leave your head instead of coming home with you. Optional argument: a Permanence stream/project name, OR an absolute path to a registered project — either works, for sessions with no meaningful working folder (desktop AI apps). Trigger phrases: "good night Permanence", "night Permanence". ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-shutdown.md) · ★1)
- **perma-startup**: Morning bookend to /perma-shutdown — project-specific "where you left off" plus a small (2-3) set of next-task options for the current repo's Permanence stream. Optional argument: a Permanence stream/project name, OR an absolute path to a registered project — either works, for sessions with no meaningful working folder (desktop AI apps) or to switch projects mid-session. Trigger phrases: "good morning Permanence", "morning Permanence". ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-startup.md) · ★1)
